## Text Elements
If we don't insert the delay, it might happen that for some frequencies, destructive interference occurs, which would make the music sound off.
Other effects such as comb filtering, Hass effect and Franssen effect can also occur, where the amount of delay distorts the overall signal.
In this case, with a delay of 0.116, we would hear the Hass effect, where an "echo" sound can be heard. ^XV8aEGPr
